```
        var totalLabel = document.getElementById('flat-total-records');
        if (totalLabel) totalLabel.textContent = 'Total: ' + total;
        
        var start = (page - 1) * flatItemsPerPage;
        var end = Math.min(start + flatItemsPerPage, total);
        
        for (var i = start; i < end; i++) {
            var item = flatCatalog[i];
            var tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.setAttribute('data-id', item.id || (item.clave + '-' + i));
            
            var reqPruebas = (item.pruebas_incluidas && item.pruebas_incluidas.trim() !== '') ? item.pruebas_incluidas.trim().replace(/\n/g, '<br>') : '<span style="color:#999; font-style:italic;">Consultar en LAESH</span>';
            var reqPrep = (item.preparacion && item.preparacion.trim() !== '') ? item.preparacion.trim() : '<span style="color:#999; font-style:italic;">Consultar en LAESH</span>';

            var reqTiempo = (item.tiempo && item.tiempo.trim() !== '') ? item.tiempo.trim().replace(/\.0$/, '') : '<span style="color:#999; font-style:italic;">Consultar en LAESH</span>';
            var reqClave = (item.clave && item.clave.trim() !== '') ? item.clave.trim().replace(/\.0$/, '') : '';
            
            tr.innerHTML = `

                <td>${reqClave}</td>

            tbody.appendChild(tr);

        renderFlatPagination(total, page);
```

```
    function resetSettingsMenu() {
        if(menu20EstMed) { menu20EstMed.classList.remove('active'); menu20EstMed.style.color = 'var(--text-muted)'; }
        if(menuGabinetes) { menuGabinetes.classList.remove('active'); menuGabinetes.style.color = 'var(--text-muted)'; }
        if(menuIGabinetes) { menuIGabinetes.classList.remove('active'); menuIGabinetes.style.color = 'var(--text-muted)'; }

        if(viewGabinetes) viewGabinetes.style.display = 'none';

    if (menu20EstMed) {
        menu20EstMed.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            resetSettingsMenu();
            menu20EstMed.classList.add('active');
            menu20EstMed.style.color = 'var(--primary-dark)';
            if(view20EstMed) view20EstMed.style.display = 'flex';
        });

            menuGabinetes.style.color = 'var(--primary-dark)';
            if(viewGabinetes) viewGabinetes.style.display = 'grid';
```

```
    var menu20EstMed = document.getElementById('menu-20estmed');
    var menuGabinetes = document.getElementById('menu-gabinetes');
    var menuIGabinetes = document.getElementById('menu-igabinetes');
    var view20EstMed = document.getElementById('settings-20estmed-view');
    var viewGabinetes = document.getElementById('settings-gabinetes-view');

    function resetSettingsMenu() {
        if(menu20EstMed) { menu20EstMed.classList.remove('active'); menu20EstMed.style.color = 'var(--text-muted)'; }
        if(menuGabinetes) { menuGabinetes.classList.remove('active'); menuGabinetes.style.color = 'var(--text-muted)'; }
        if(menuIGabinetes) { menuIGabinetes.classList.remove('active'); menuIGabinetes.style.color = 'var(--text-muted)'; }
        if(view20EstMed) view20EstMed.style.display = 'none';
        if(viewGabinetes) viewGabinetes.style.display = 'none';
    }

    if (menu20EstMed) {
        menu20EstMed.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            resetSettingsMenu();
            menu20EstMed.classList.add('active');
            menu20EstMed.style.color = 'var(--primary-dark)';
            if(view20EstMed) view20EstMed.style.display = 'flex';
        });
    }

    if (menuGabinetes) {
        menuGabinetes.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            resetSettingsMenu();
            menuGabinetes.classList.add('active');
            menuGabinetes.style.color = 'var(--primary-dark)';
            if(viewGabinetes) viewGabinetes.style.display = 'grid';
        });
    }

    if (menuIGabinetes) {
        menuIGabinetes.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            resetSettingsMenu();
            menuIGabinetes.classList.add('active');
            menuIGabinetes.style.color = 'var(--primary-dark)';
            // TODO: show IGabinetes view when created
        });
    }
```

```
            // Add Row (Mockup logic)
            var btnAdd = target.closest('.btn-add-row');
            if (btnAdd) {
                var listId = btnAdd.getAttribute('data-target');
                var container = document.getElementById(listId);
                if (container) {
                    var isGabinete = listId === 'list-gabinetes';
                    var newRow = document.createElement('div');
                    newRow.className = 'row-item';
                    newRow.innerHTML = `
                        <input type="text" class="form-input form-input--bg new-row-input" placeholder="Nombre..." style="width: 200px; padding: 4px 8px; font-size: 0.95rem;">
                        <div style="display: flex; gap: 0.5rem; align-items: center;">
                            <span class="action-icon btn-save-row" style="cursor: pointer; color: #10b981;" title="Guardar">✔️</span>
                            <span class="action-icon btn-cancel-row" style="cursor: pointer; color: #ef4444;" title="Cancelar">❌</span>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    container.appendChild(newRow);
                    var input = newRow.querySelector('.new-row-input');
                    input.focus();
                    
                    var saveFn = function() {
                        var val = input.value.trim();
                        if (val === '') {
                            newRow.remove();
                            return;
                        }
                        newRow.innerHTML = `
                            <span class="row-name" style="font-weight: 500;">${val}</span>
                            <div style="display: flex; gap: 0.5rem; align-items: center;">
                                <span class="action-icon btn-edit-row" style="cursor: pointer; color: var(--primary);" title="Editar">✏️</span>
                                <span class="action-icon btn-delete-row" style="cursor: pointer; color: #ef4444;" title="Eliminar">❌</span>
                                ${isGabinete ? '<span class="action-icon btn-ver-subgabinetes" style="cursor: pointer; color: var(--primary-dark);" title="Ver Subgabinetes">📋</span>' : ''}
                                <input type="checkbox" class="chk-activar-paneles" data-tipo="${isGabinete ? 'Gabinete' : 'Sub Gabinete'}" data-nombre="${val}" style="cursor: pointer; transform: scale(1.2);">
                            </div>
                        `;
                    };
                    
                    newRow.querySelector('.btn-save-row').addEventListener('click', saveFn);
                    newRow.querySelector('.btn-cancel-row').addEventListener('click', function() { newRow.remove(); });
                    input.addEventListener('keypress', function(ev) { if (ev.key === 'Enter') saveFn(); });
                }
            }
        });
    }

    // Autocomplete Logic for Panel C
    var settingsSearchNombre = document.getElementById('settings-search-nombre');
    var settingsSearchGrupo = document.getElementById('settings-search-grupo');
    var settingsResults = document.getElementById('settings-autocomplete-results');
    var settingsSelectedTags = document.getElementById('settings-selected-tags');
    var settingsTagsEmpty = document.getElementById('settings-tags-empty');

    function renderSettingsAutocomplete(results) {
        if (!settingsResults) return;
        settingsResults.innerHTML = '';
        if (results.length === 0) {
            settingsResults.style.display = 'none';
            return;
        }
        
        var ul = document.createElement('ul');
        ul.style.listStyle = 'none';
        ul.style.margin = '0';
        ul.style.padding = '0';
        
        // Limit to 50
        var max = Math.min(results.length, 50);
        for(var i=0; i<max; i++) {
            var item = results[i];
            var li = document.createElement('li');
            li.style.padding = '8px 12px';
            li.style.borderBottom = '1px solid #f1f5f9';
            li.style.cursor = 'pointer';
            li.style.fontSize = '0.9rem';
            li.innerHTML = `<strong>${item.nombre}</strong> <span style="color:#64748b; font-size:0.8rem;">(${item.clave})</span>`;
            
            li.addEventListener('mouseenter', function(e) { e.target.style.background = '#f8fafc'; });
            li.addEventListener('mouseleave', function(e) { e.target.style.background = 'transparent'; });
            
            (function(study) {
                li.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    addStudyTag(study);
                    settingsResults.style.display = 'none';
                    if (settingsSearchNombre) settingsSearchNombre.value = '';
                    if (settingsSearchGrupo) settingsSearchGrupo.value = '';
                });
```

```
                        <span class="action-icon btn-cancel-edit" style="cursor: pointer; color: #ef4444;" title="Cancelar">❌</span>
                    </div>
                `;
                
                // Prepend so it appears exactly where it should be
                row.insertBefore(editUi, row.firstChild);
                var input = editUi.querySelector('.edit-row-input');
                input.focus();
                
                var closeEditFn = function() {
                    editUi.remove();
                    spanName.style.display = '';
                    actionsDiv.style.display = 'flex';
                };
                
                var saveEditFn = function() {
                    var val = input.value.trim();
                    if (val === '') {
                        closeEditFn();
                        return;
                    }
                    spanName.textContent = val;
                    var chk = row.querySelector('.chk-activar-paneles');
                    if (chk) chk.setAttribute('data-nombre', val);
                    closeEditFn();
                    checkPanelesCD(); // update panel CD title if this row is currently active
                };
                
                editUi.querySelector('.btn-save-edit').addEventListener('click', saveEditFn);
                editUi.querySelector('.btn-cancel-edit').addEventListener('click', closeEditFn);
                input.addEventListener('keypress', function(ev) { if (ev.key === 'Enter') saveEditFn(); });
            }
            
            // Add Row (Mockup logic)
            var btnAdd = target.closest('.btn-add-row');
            if (btnAdd) {
                var listId = btnAdd.getAttribute('data-target');
                var container = document.getElementById(listId);
                if (container) {
                    var isGabinete = listId === 'list-gabinetes';
                    var newRow = document.createElement('div');
                    newRow.className = 'row-item';
                    newRow.innerHTML = `
                        <input type="text" class="form-input form-input--bg new-row-input" placeholder="Nombre..." style="width: 200px; padding: 4px 8px; font-size: 0.95rem;">
                        <div style="display: flex; gap: 0.5rem; align-items: center;">
                            <span class="action-icon btn-save-row" style="cursor: pointer; color: #10b981;" title="Guardar">✔️</span>
                            <span class="action-icon btn-cancel-row" style="cursor: pointer; color: #ef4444;" title="Cancelar">❌</span>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    container.appendChild(newRow);
                    var input = newRow.querySelector('.new-row-input');
                    input.focus();
                    
                    var saveFn = function() {
                        var val = input.value.trim();
                        if (val === '') {
                            newRow.remove();
                            return;
                        }
                        newRow.innerHTML = `
                            <span class="row-name" style="font-weight: 500;">${val}</span>
                            <div style="display: flex; gap: 0.5rem; align-items: center;">
                                <span class="action-icon btn-edit-row" style="cursor: pointer; color: var(--primary);" title="Editar">✏️</span>
                                <span class="action-icon btn-delete-row" style="cursor: pointer; color: #ef4444;" title="Eliminar">❌</span>
                                ${isGabinete ? '<span class="action-icon btn-ver-subgabinetes" style="cursor: pointer; color: var(--primary-dark);" title="Ver Subgabinetes">📋</span>' : ''}
                                <input type="checkbox" class="chk-activar-paneles" data-tipo="${isGabinete ? 'Gabinete' : 'Sub Gabinete'}" data-nombre="${val}" style="cursor: pointer; transform: scale(1.2);">
                            </div>
                        `;
                    };
                    
                    newRow.querySelector('.btn-save-row').addEventListener('click', saveFn);
                    newRow.querySelector('.btn-cancel-row').addEventListener('click', function() { newRow.remove(); });
                    input.addEventListener('keypress', function(ev) { if (ev.key === 'Enter') saveFn(); });
                }
            }
        });
    }
```

```
        // Drag and Drop Logic for Categories
        var dragSrcEl = null;
        content.addEventListener('dragstart', function(e) {
            if (e.target.classList && e.target.classList.contains('builder-cat-card')) {
                dragSrcEl = e.target;
                e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
                e.dataTransfer.setData('text/html', e.target.innerHTML);
                e.target.style.opacity = '0.4';
                e.target.removeAttribute('open'); // Collapse while dragging
            }
        });
        content.addEventListener('dragover', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';
            var target = e.target.closest('.builder-cat-card');
            if (target && target !== dragSrcEl) {
                target.style.borderTop = '3px solid var(--primary)';
            }
            return false;
        });
        content.addEventListener('dragleave', function(e) {
            var target = e.target.closest('.builder-cat-card');
            if (target && target !== dragSrcEl) {
                target.style.borderTop = '1px solid var(--border)';
            }
        });
        content.addEventListener('drop', function(e) {
            e.stopPropagation();
            var target = e.target.closest('.builder-cat-card');
            if (target && dragSrcEl && target !== dragSrcEl) {
                target.style.borderTop = '1px solid var(--border)';
                var container = document.getElementById('categorias-container');
                var targetRect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
                if (e.clientY < targetRect.top + targetRect.height / 2) {
                    container.insertBefore(dragSrcEl, target);
                } else {
                    container.insertBefore(dragSrcEl, target.nextSibling);
                }
            }
            return false;
        });
```

```
    if (searchIGab) {
        searchIGab.addEventListener('input', function() {
            var term = this.value.toLowerCase().trim();
            if (term.length < 2) {
                autocompleteIGab.style.display = 'none';
                return;
            }

            var results = mockGabinetesList.filter(function(item) {
                return item.nombre.toLowerCase().includes(term) && !currentIGabinetesLinked.some(l => l.id === item.id);
            });

            if (results.length > 0) {
                var html = results.map(function(item) {
                    var color = item.tipo === 'Gabinete' ? '#3b82f6' : '#10b981';
                    return `
                        <div class="iautocomplete-item" data-id="${item.id}" style="padding: 10px 14px; cursor: pointer; border-bottom: 1px solid #f1f5f9; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between;">
                            <span style="font-weight: 500; color: #1e293b;">${item.nombre}</span>
                            <span style="font-size: 0.75rem; background: ${color}20; color: ${color}; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 4px; font-weight: bold;">${item.tipo}</span>
                        </div>
                    `;
                }).join('');
                autocompleteIGab.innerHTML = html;
                autocompleteIGab.style.display = 'block';

                document.querySelectorAll('.iautocomplete-item').forEach(function(el) {
                    el.addEventListener('click', function() {
                        var id = parseInt(this.getAttribute('data-id'), 10);
                        var itemToAdd = mockGabinetesList.find(i => i.id === id);
                        if (itemToAdd) {
                            currentIGabinetesLinked.push(itemToAdd);
                            isIGabinetesDirty = true;
                            renderIGabTags();
                            updateIGabSaveBtn();
                        }
                        searchIGab.value = '';
                        autocompleteIGab.style.display = 'none';
                    });
                });
            } else {
                autocompleteIGab.innerHTML = '<div style="padding: 10px 14px; color: #94a3b8; font-style: italic;">No se encontraron gabinetes/subgabinetes.</div>';
                autocompleteIGab.style.display = 'block';
            }
        });

        document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            if (autocompleteIGab && !autocompleteIGab.contains(e.target) && e.target !== searchIGab) {
                autocompleteIGab.style.display = 'none';
            }
        });
    }

    if (btnSaveIGab) {
        btnSaveIGab.addEventListener('click', function() {
            if (btnSaveIGab.disabled) return;
            var originalHtml = btnSaveIGab.innerHTML;
            btnSaveIGab.innerHTML = `<svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12"></polyline></svg> ¡Guardado!`;
            btnSaveIGab.style.background = '#059669'; // darker green
```

```
                </div>
            `;
```

```
    function renderFlatTable(page) {
        currentFlatPage = page;
        var tbody = document.querySelector('#flat-catalog-table tbody');
        tbody.innerHTML = '';
        
        var total = flatCatalog.length;
        var totalLabel = document.getElementById('flat-total-records');
        if (totalLabel) totalLabel.textContent = 'Total: ' + total;
        
        var start = (page - 1) * flatItemsPerPage;
        var end = Math.min(start + flatItemsPerPage, total);
        
        for (var i = start; i < end; i++) {
            var item = flatCatalog[i];
            var tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.setAttribute('data-id', item.id || (item.clave + '-' + i));
            
            var reqPruebas = (item.pruebas_incluidas && item.pruebas_incluidas.trim() !== '') ? item.pruebas_incluidas.trim().replace(/\n/g, '<br>') : '<span style="color:#999; font-style:italic;">Consultar en LAESH</span>';
            var reqPrep = (item.preparacion && item.preparacion.trim() !== '') ? item.preparacion.trim() : '<span style="color:#999; font-style:italic;">Consultar en LAESH</span>';
            var reqMuestra = (item.muestra && item.muestra.trim() !== '') ? item.muestra.trim() : '<span style="color:#999; font-style:italic;">Consultar en LAESH</span>';
            var reqContenedor = (item.contenedor && item.contenedor.trim() !== '') ? item.contenedor.trim() : '<span style="color:#999; font-style:italic;">Consultar en LAESH</span>';
            var reqTiempo = (item.tiempo && item.tiempo.trim() !== '') ? item.tiempo.trim().replace(/\.0$/, '') : '<span style="color:#999; font-style:italic;">Consultar en LAESH</span>';
            var reqClave = (item.clave && item.clave.trim() !== '') ? item.clave.trim().replace(/\.0$/, '') : '';
            
            tr.innerHTML = `
                <td><strong>${i + 1}</strong></td>
                <td>${reqClave}</td>
                <td style="white-space: normal; min-width: 250px;"><strong>${item.nombre || ''}</strong></td>
                <td>${reqMuestra}</td>
                <td>${reqContenedor}</td>
                <td>${reqTiempo}</td>
                <td style="white-space: normal; min-width: 200px;">${item.categoriaNombre || ''}</td>
                <td style="white-space: normal; min-width: 200px;">${reqPrep}</td>
                <td style="white-space: normal; min-width: 300px;"><div style="max-height:80px; overflow-y:auto; font-size:0.85em;">${reqPruebas}</div></td>
            `;
            tbody.appendChild(tr);
        }
        
        renderFlatPagination(total, page);
    }

    function renderFlatPagination(total, currentPage) {
        var pag = document.getElementById('flat-pagination');
        pag.innerHTML = '';
        var totalPages = Math.ceil(total / flatItemsPerPage);
        
        if (totalPages <= 1) {
            pag.style.display = 'none';
            return;
        }
        pag.style.display = 'flex';
        
        var maxButtons = 5;
```

I'm currently focused on constructing table rows with editable cells. I'm dynamically generating the HTML for each row, including cells for clave, nombre, muestra, contenedor, and tiempo. Each cell is contenteditable, with data attributes for easy tracking. I am implementing strong labels and preserving white space. Each cell also contains the data-orig values to maintain the unmodified original values for comparison.


**Implementing HTML Escaping**

I've realized that HTML escaping is critical, and I am now integrating `escapeHtml` into the editable cell generation to prevent potential XSS vulnerabilities. This is particularly crucial for the editable fields like `categoriaNombre`, `preparacion`, and `pruebas_incluidas`, where user input could introduce malicious HTML. I am now incorporating this for all dynamic data injection.


**Optimizing Data Handling**

I'm now streamlining data handling within the editable cells. Initially, I considered a helper function but opted to utilize JavaScript's `dataset` for storing raw data directly. While `innerHTML` offers faster creation, I am relying on the `input` event to efficiently read `textContent` instead. This is streamlining HTML generation and focusing data access.


**Enhancing Editable Cells**

I'm now focusing on integrating event listeners for the editable cells within the flat table. Specifically, I am writing event listeners for focus, blur, and modification tracking. These listeners will be crucial for capturing and managing changes made to the cell data. I'm appending this after the `renderFlatTable` function to ensure proper integration with the table generation process.


**Refining Prueba Display**

I'm now refining the display of 'pruebas' within the editable cells, particularly handling the comma-separated or newline-separated values. On focus, I show the raw text; on blur, I parse it and render either a formatted unordered list or a placeholder indicating no data, or to consult in LAESH. This event-driven approach ensures a user-friendly and informative cell presentation.



**Tracking Cell Modifications**

I am now tracking changes in editable cells using an `input` event listener, which triggers a visual cue, such as a green outline and a light background color, for modified cells. This is updating an ID that is tracking the changes, and will trigger updates in related counts. Further, a save button is enabled only when there are edits. I'm streamlining the data modification process.


**Enabling Save Button**

I've added the logic to enable the save button when changes are made. Now, the save button appears when a user edits a cell, offering a clear indication that data needs to be persisted. This ensures the workflow is more intuitive and responsive to user actions. I'm leveraging the update tracker to manage the 'Save' button state.


**Implementing Row Creation**

I'm now integrating the row creation feature. Once a new item is generated, I'm dynamically focusing the name cell of that new row. I'm also ensuring that the table scrolls to center the new row for improved visibility. Further, I'm enabling related components in the UI upon row creation. I am focusing on seamless user experience for new item additions.


**Simulating Save Actions**

I'm now simulating the save action for the flat table. Upon button click, I'm clearing visual cues indicating the edits have been "saved" - such as the green outlines, etc. In addition, I am clearing the modifications tracker. I'm also changing the button's display and updating the record count accordingly. The changes simulate a successful data save.

```
        pag.innerHTML = '';
        var totalPages = Math.ceil(total / flatItemsPerPage);
        
        if (totalPages <= 1) {
            pag.style.display = 'none';
            return;
        }
        pag.style.display = 'flex';
        
        var maxButtons = 5;
        var startPage = Math.max(1, currentPage - Math.floor(maxButtons / 2));
        var endPage = Math.min(totalPages, startPage + maxButtons - 1);
        
        if (endPage - startPage + 1 < maxButtons) {
            startPage = Math.max(1, endPage - maxButtons + 1);
        }
        
        var baseStyle = "background: transparent; border: none; color: #666; cursor: pointer; padding: 0.25rem 0.5rem; font-size: 0.9rem;";
        var activeStyle = "background: transparent; border: none; color: #000; font-weight: bold; cursor: pointer; padding: 0.25rem 0.5rem; font-size: 1rem;";

        if (currentPage > 1) {
            var btnPrev = document.createElement('button');
            btnPrev.type = 'button';
            btnPrev.style = baseStyle;
            btnPrev.textContent = '<<';
            btnPrev.onclick = function() { renderFlatTable(Math.max(1, currentPage - 5)); };
            pag.appendChild(btnPrev);
        }
        
        for (var i = startPage; i <= endPage; i++) {
            var btn = document.createElement('button');
            btn.type = 'button';
            btn.style = i === currentPage ? activeStyle : baseStyle;
            btn.textContent = i;
            (function(p) {
                btn.onclick = function() { renderFlatTable(p); };
            })(i);
            pag.appendChild(btn);
        }
        
        if (currentPage < totalPages) {
            var btnNext = document.createElement('button');
            btnNext.type = 'button';
            btnNext.style = baseStyle;
            btnNext.textContent = '>>';
            btnNext.onclick = function() { renderFlatTable(Math.min(totalPages, currentPage + 5)); };
            pag.appendChild(btnNext);
        }
    }

    // Flat Autocomplete
    var searchNombre = document.getElementById('flat-search-nombre');
    var searchGrupo = document.getElementById('flat-search-grupo');
    var flatResults = document.getElementById('flat-autocomplete-results');
    
    function handleFlatSearch() {
        var nTerm = searchNombre.value.toLowerCase();
        var gTerm = searchGrupo.value.toLowerCase();
        
        if (nTerm.length < 2 && gTerm.length < 2) {
            flatResults.style.display = 'none';
            return;
        }
        
        var matches = flatCatalog.filter(function(item) {
            var mN = true, mG = true;
            if (nTerm.length >= 2) {
                mN = (item.nombre || '').toLowerCase().includes(nTerm);
            }
            if (gTerm.length >= 2) {
                mG = (item.grupoNombre || '').toLowerCase().includes(gTerm);
            }
            return mN && mG;
        }).slice(0, 15);
        
        if (matches.length === 0) {
            flatResults.innerHTML = '<div style="padding: 0.5rem; color: #666;">Sin resultados</div>';
            flatResults.style.display = 'block';
            return;
        }
        
        flatResults.innerHTML = '';
        matches.forEach(function(m) {
            var div = document.createElement('div');
            div.style.padding = '0.5rem';
            div.style.borderBottom = '1px solid var(--border)';
            div.style.cursor = 'pointer';
            div.innerHTML = `<strong>${m.nombre}</strong> <span style="color:#666; font-size:0.85em;">- ${m.grupoNombre} / ${m.categoriaNombre}</span>`;
            
            div.addEventListener('mouseenter', function() { div.style.backgroundColor = '#f1f5f9'; });
            div.addEventListener('mouseleave', function() { div.style.backgroundColor = 'transparent'; });
            
            div.addEventListener('click', function() {
                var uniqueId = m.id || (m.clave + '-' + flatCatalog.indexOf(m));
                var globalIndex = flatCatalog.findIndex(x => (x.id || (x.clave + '-' + flatCatalog.indexOf(x))) === uniqueId);
                if (globalIndex !== -1) {
                    var targetPage = Math.floor(globalIndex / flatItemsPerPage) + 1;
                    renderFlatTable(targetPage);
                    setTimeout(function() {
                        var tr = document.querySelector('#flat-catalog-table tbody tr[data-id="' + uniqueId + '"]');
                        if (tr) {
                            tr.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
                            tr.classList.add('highlight-flash');
                            setTimeout(function() { tr.classList.remove('highlight-flash'); }, 3000);
                        }
                    }, 100);
                }
                flatResults.style.display = 'none';
                searchNombre.value = '';
                searchGrupo.value = '';
            });
```

```
/**
 * cms-dirty-tracker.js — v2 — Módulo unificado de rastreo, persistencia y protección de cambios en CMS
 *
 * Responsabilidades:
 * 1. Inicializa baseline (dataset.original) para inputs, textareas, selects y CKEditor 5.
 * 2. Muestra/remueve punto rojo (.cms-field-dirty-dot) en la esquina superior izquierda del campo.
 * 3. Sincroniza el contador exacto del badge (.tab-change-badge) en la pestaña correspondiente.
 * 4. [v2] beforeunload Guard: intercepta cierre/refresh de pestaña si hay cambios sin publicar.
 * 5. [v2] Draft Auto-Save: persiste cambios en localStorage en tiempo real por panel.
 * 6. [v2] Draft Restore: al cargar la página, detecta borradores y ofrece restaurarlos.
 * 7. Resetea el estado (indicadores + draft localStorage) tras una publicación exitosa.
 */
(function(window, document) {
    'use strict';

    var TRACK_SELECTOR = 'input[name]:not([type="file"]), textarea[name], select[name]';
    var DRAFT_PREFIX   = 'cms_draft_';   // Clave en localStorage: cms_draft_{panelId}
    var DRAFT_MAX_AGE  = 7 * 86400 * 1000; // 7 días en ms — borradores más viejos se descartan
    var activePanelIds = [
        'panel-hero',
        'panel-quienes-somos',
        'panel-especialidades',
        'panel-promociones',
        'panel-calidad',
        'panel-ubicacion',
        'panel-footer',
        'panel-aviso-privacidad',
        'panel-video-promo',
        'panel-configuracion-general',
        'panel-seo'
    ];

    // Bandera global: ¿hay AL MENOS UN campo sucio en cualquier panel activo?
    var _hasDirtyFields = false;
    // Bandera de persistencia local: false = solo guard + dots/badges, sin tocar localStorage
    var _draftEnabled   = true;

    var CmsDirtyTracker = {

        // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        //  INIT
        // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

        /**
         * Inicializa la supervisión en los paneles especificados o por defecto.
         * @param {string[]} panelIds   IDs de paneles a monitorear
         * @param {object}   [options]  Opciones:
         *   enableDraft {boolean} — true (default): activa Auto-Save + Restore en localStorage.
         *                           false: solo activa guard beforeunload + dots/badges (sin localStorage).
         */
        init: function(panelIds, options) {
            if (Array.isArray(panelIds)) {
                activePanelIds = panelIds;
            }
            var opts = options || {};
            _draftEnabled = (opts.enableDraft !== false); // default true; false para deshabilitar localStorage
            var self = this;

            // 1. Restaurar borradores (solo si persistencia habilitada)
            if (_draftEnabled) {
                self._restoreDraftsIfAny();
            }

            // 2. Inicializar baseline de cada panel
            activePanelIds.forEach(function(panelId) {

            });

                if (self.shouldTrack(e.target)) self.evalField(e.target);
            });

            self._installBeforeUnloadGuard();

            if (!panel || !panel.id) return false;
            return activePanelIds.indexOf(panel.id) !== -1;
```

```
    if (settingsGabinetesView) {
        // Evento click para el botón guardar vinculaciones
        settingsGabinetesView.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            var btnSave = e.target.closest('#btn-save-vinculaciones');
            if (btnSave && !btnSave.disabled) {
                // Mockup: Simulate AJAX save
                var originalHtml = btnSave.innerHTML;
                btnSave.innerHTML = `<svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12"></polyline></svg> ¡Guardado!`;
                btnSave.style.background = '#059669'; // darker green for success
                isPanelDDirty = false;
                setTimeout(function() {
                    updateSaveButtonState();
                    btnSave.innerHTML = originalHtml;
                }, 1500);
            }
        });

        settingsGabinetesView.addEventListener('change', function(e) {
            if (e.target.classList.contains('chk-activar-paneles')) {
                // Check dirty state of Panel D
                if (isPanelDDirty) {
                    if (!confirm("Hay cambios sin guardar en los estudios vinculados (Panel D). ¿Deseas descartar estos cambios y continuar?")) {
                        // Revert the checkbox toggle
                        e.target.checked = !e.target.checked;
                        return;
                    }
                    // Reset dirty state if they agree to discard
                    isPanelDDirty = false;
                    updateSaveButtonState();
                }

                // Global Exclusivity
                if (e.target.checked) {
                    var currentChk = e.target;
                    document.querySelectorAll('.chk-activar-paneles').forEach(function(chk) {
                        if (chk !== currentChk) chk.checked = false;
                    });
                }
                checkPanelesCD();
            }
        });

        function handleRowActions(e) {
            var target = e.target;
            
            // Momentary flash for action icons
            var actionIcon = target.closest('.action-icon:not(.btn-ver-subgabinetes)');
            if (actionIcon) {
                actionIcon.classList.add('active');
                setTimeout(function() { actionIcon.classList.remove('active'); }, 200);
            }
            
            // Show/Hide Panel B (List Icon)
            var btnVer = target.closest('.btn-ver-subgabinetes');
            if (btnVer) {
                var isActive = btnVer.classList.contains('active');
                document.querySelectorAll('.btn-ver-subgabinetes').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
                
                if (isActive) {
                    if (panelB) {
                        panelB.style.display = 'none';
                        panelB.querySelectorAll('.chk-activar-paneles').forEach(chk => chk.checked = false);
                        checkPanelesCD();
                    }
                } else {
                    btnVer.classList.add('active');
                    if (panelB) panelB.style.display = 'flex';
                }
            }
            
            // Delete Row
            var btnDelete = target.closest('.btn-delete-row');
            if (btnDelete) {
                var row = btnDelete.closest('.row-item');
                var nombre = row.querySelector('.row-name') ? row.querySelector('.row-name').textContent : 'este registro';
                if (confirm('¿Estás seguro de que deseas eliminar ' + nombre + '?')) {
                    var isIGab = !!row.closest('#settings-igabinetes-view');
                    row.remove();
                    if (isIGab) {
                        if (typeof currentSelectedIGabinete !== 'undefined' && currentSelectedIGabinete === nombre) {
                            currentSelectedIGabinete = null;
                            if (document.getElementById('panel-igabinetes-cd-container')) {
                                document.getElementById('panel-igabinetes-cd-container').style.display = 'none';
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        checkPanelesCD();
                    }
                }
            }

            // Edit Row
            var btnEdit = target.closest('.btn-edit-row');
            if (btnEdit) {
                var row = btnEdit.closest('.row-item');
                var isIGab = !!row.closest('#settings-igabinetes-view');
                var spanName = row.querySelector('.row-name');
                var actionsDiv = row.querySelector('div'); // The flex container for icons
                var currentName = spanName.textContent;
                
                // Hide view elements
                spanName.style.display = 'none';
                actionsDiv.style.display = 'none';
                
                // Create Edit UI
                var editUi = document.createElement('div');
                editUi.className = 'edit-ui';
                editUi.style = 'display: flex; width: 100%; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;';
                editUi.innerHTML = `
                    <input type="text" class="form-input form-input--bg edit-row-input" value="${currentName}" style="width: 200px; padding: 4px 8px; font-size: 0.95rem;">
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 0.5rem; align-items: center;">
                        <span class="action-icon btn-save-edit" style="cursor: pointer; color: #10b981;" title="Guardar">✔️</span>
                        <span class="action-icon btn-cancel-edit" style="cursor: pointer; color: #ef4444;" title="Cancelar">❌</span>
                    </div>
                `;
                
                // Prepend so it appears exactly where it should be
                row.insertBefore(editUi, row.firstChild);
                var input = editUi.querySelector('.edit-row-input');
```

```
    btnSaveFull.addEventListener('click', function() {
        // Guardar estado del grupo activo primero
        saveCurrentDOMToTree();
        // Normalizar el campo 'orden' de TODOS los grupos según su posición
        // en el array (= orden visual de las pestañas de izq. a der.)
        catalogTree.forEach(function(grp, idx) { grp.orden = idx + 1; });
        var csrf = document.querySelector('input[name="csrf_token"]');
        var token = csrf ? csrf.value : '';
        
        btnSaveFull.disabled = true;
        btnSaveFull.textContent = 'Guardando...';
        
        fetch('/laesh/rc/api/catalogos', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'X-CSRF-TOKEN': token
            },
            body: JSON.stringify(catalogTree)
        })
        .then(res => res.json())
        .then(data => {
            if (data.success) {
                if(window.showToast) showToast(data.mensaje, 'success');
                // Reload tree from server to get new IDs
                loadCatalogTree();
            } else {
                if(window.showToast) showToast(data.error || 'Error al guardar', 'error');
            }
        })
        .catch(err => {
            console.error('Error', err);
            if(window.showToast) showToast('Error de red al guardar', 'error');
        })
        .finally(() => {
            btnSaveFull.disabled = false;
            btnSaveFull.textContent = '💾 Guardar Estructura';
        });
    });

    // Autocompletado y Búsqueda Fantasma
    var searchInput = document.getElementById('builder-search-input');
    var searchResults = document.getElementById('builder-search-results');

            var q = this.value.toLowerCase().trim();
            searchResults.innerHTML = '';

                searchResults.style.display = 'none';
```

```
/* ── MÓDULO CONSTRUCTOR DE CATÁLOGOS (Abanicos Jerárquicos) ── */
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    var btnViewTable = document.getElementById('btn-view-table');
    var btnViewBuilder = document.getElementById('btn-view-builder');
    var btnViewSettings = document.getElementById('btn-view-settings');
    var viewBuilder = document.getElementById('view-catalog-builder');
    var viewSettings = document.getElementById('view-catalog-settings');
    var btnSaveFull = document.getElementById('btn-guardar-catalogo-full');
    var btnAddEstudio = document.getElementById('btn-agregar-estudio');
    
    if (!viewBuilder) return;

    var viewTable = document.getElementById('view-catalog-table');
    var builderSearchContainer = document.getElementById('builder-search-container');
    var flatSearchContainer = document.getElementById('flat-search-container');
    var settingsMenuContainer = document.getElementById('settings-menu-container');
    var flatPagination = document.getElementById('flat-pagination');
    
    function resetActiveButtons() {
        if (btnViewTable) { btnViewTable.classList.remove('active'); btnViewTable.classList.add('btn-secondary'); }
        if (btnViewBuilder) { btnViewBuilder.classList.remove('active'); btnViewBuilder.classList.add('btn-secondary'); }
        if (btnViewSettings) { btnViewSettings.classList.remove('active'); btnViewSettings.classList.add('btn-secondary'); }
    }

    function hideAllViews() {
        if (viewBuilder) viewBuilder.classList.add('d-none');
        if (viewTable) viewTable.classList.add('d-none');
        if (viewSettings) viewSettings.classList.add('d-none');
        
        if (builderSearchContainer) builderSearchContainer.style.display = 'none';
        if (flatSearchContainer) flatSearchContainer.style.display = 'none';
        if (settingsMenuContainer) settingsMenuContainer.style.display = 'none';
        if (flatPagination) flatPagination.style.display = 'none';
        
        if (btnSaveFull) btnSaveFull.style.display = 'none';
        if (btnAddEstudio) btnAddEstudio.style.display = 'none';
    }

    if (btnViewTable) {
        btnViewTable.addEventListener('click', function() {
            hideAllViews();
            resetActiveButtons();
            viewTable.classList.remove('d-none');
            btnViewTable.classList.remove('btn-secondary');
            btnViewTable.classList.add('active');
            
            if (flatSearchContainer) flatSearchContainer.style.display = 'flex';
            
            if (flatPagination && flatCatalog.length > 0 && Math.ceil(flatCatalog.length / flatItemsPerPage) > 1) {
                flatPagination.style.display = 'flex';
            }
            var flatActions = document.getElementById('flat-table-actions');
            if (flatActions) flatActions.style.display = 'flex';
            if (flatCatalog.length === 0 && catalogTree.length > 0) {
                flattenCatalogTree();
                renderFlatTable(1);
            }
        });
    }

    if (btnViewBuilder) {
        btnViewBuilder.addEventListener('click', function() {
            hideAllViews();
            resetActiveButtons();
            viewBuilder.classList.remove('d-none');
            btnViewBuilder.classList.remove('btn-secondary');
            btnViewBuilder.classList.add('active');
            
            if (builderSearchContainer) builderSearchContainer.style.display = 'block';
            if (btnSaveFull) btnSaveFull.style.display = 'inline-block';
        });
    }

    // --- Navigation Settings Submenu ---
    var menu20EstMed = document.getElementById('menu-20estmed');
    var menuGabinetes = document.getElementById('menu-gabinetes');
    var menuIGabinetes = document.getElementById('menu-igabinetes');
    var view20EstMed = document.getElementById('settings-20estmed-view');
    var viewGabinetes = document.getElementById('settings-gabinetes-view');
    var viewIGabinetes = document.getElementById('settings-igabinetes-view');

    function resetSettingsMenu() {
        if(menu20EstMed) { menu20EstMed.classList.remove('active'); menu20EstMed.style.color = 'var(--text-muted)'; }
        if(menuGabinetes) { menuGabinetes.classList.remove('active'); menuGabinetes.style.color = 'var(--text-muted)'; }
        if(menuIGabinetes) { menuIGabinetes.classList.remove('active'); menuIGabinetes.style.color = 'var(--text-muted)'; }
        if(view20EstMed) view20EstMed.style.display = 'none';
        if(viewGabinetes) viewGabinetes.style.display = 'none';
        if(viewIGabinetes) viewIGabinetes.style.display = 'none';
    }

    if (menu20EstMed) {
        menu20EstMed.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            resetSettingsMenu();
            menu20EstMed.classList.add('active');
            menu20EstMed.style.color = 'var(--primary-dark)';
            if(view20EstMed) view20EstMed.style.display = 'flex';
        });
    }

    if (menuGabinetes) {
        menuGabinetes.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            resetSettingsMenu();
            menuGabinetes.classList.add('active');
            menuGabinetes.style.color = 'var(--primary-dark)';
            if(viewGabinetes) viewGabinetes.style.display = 'grid';
        });
    }

    if (menuIGabinetes) {
        menuIGabinetes.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            resetSettingsMenu();
            menuIGabinetes.classList.add('active');
            menuIGabinetes.style.color = 'var(--primary-dark)';
            if(viewIGabinetes) viewIGabinetes.style.display = 'grid';
        });
    }

    if (btnViewSettings) {
        btnViewSettings.addEventListener('click', function() {
            hideAllViews();
            resetActiveButtons();
            viewSettings.classList.remove('d-none');
            btnViewSettings.classList.remove('btn-secondary');
            btnViewSettings.classList.add('active');
            
            if (settingsMenuContainer) settingsMenuContainer.style.display = 'flex';
            
            // Force Gabinetes as default tab
            if (menuGabinetes) menuGabinetes.click();
            
            // Reset Gabinetes UI state when entering settings
            var panelB = document.getElementById('panel-gabinetes-b');
            var panelCD = document.getElementById('panel-cd-container');
            if (panelB) panelB.style.display = 'none';
            if (panelCD) panelCD.style.display = 'none';
            
            var allCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.chk-activar-paneles');
            allCheckboxes.forEach(function(chk) { chk.checked = false; });
            
            // Si el flatCatalog no está inicializado, aplanarlo
            if (!flatCatalog || flatCatalog.length === 0) {
                if (catalogTree && catalogTree.length > 0) flattenCatalogTree();
            }
            
            var allIcons = document.querySelectorAll('.action-icon');
            allIcons.forEach(function(icon) { icon.classList.remove('active'); });
        });
    }

    var catalogTree = [];
    var currentGroupIndex = 0;
    
    // Flat Table variables
    var flatCatalog = [];
    var currentFlatPage = 1;
    var flatItemsPerPage = 100;
    var flatModifiedRows = new Set();
```

```
    function focusStudyInBuilder(gIdx, cIdx, eIdx) {
        if (currentGroupIndex !== gIdx) {
            saveCurrentDOMToTree();
            currentGroupIndex = gIdx;
            renderTabs();
            renderGroupContent(currentGroupIndex);
        }
        
        var content = document.getElementById('builder-content');
        var catCards = content.querySelectorAll('.builder-cat-card');
        if (cIdx < catCards.length) {
            var catCard = catCards[cIdx];
            catCard.open = true;
            
            var estCards = catCard.querySelectorAll('.builder-est-card');
            if (eIdx < estCards.length) {
                var estCard = estCards[eIdx];
                estCard.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
                estCard.classList.add('highlight-flash');
                setTimeout(function() {
                    estCard.classList.remove('highlight-flash');
                }, 3000);
            }
        }
    }

    // ==========================================
    // FLAT TABLE LOGIC
    // ==========================================
    var currentSortCol = 'nombre';
    var currentSortDir = 'asc';

    function flattenCatalogTree() {
        flatCatalog = [];
        catalogTree.forEach(function(grupo, gIdx) {
            if (!grupo.categorias) return;
            grupo.categorias.forEach(function(cat, cIdx) {
                if (!cat.estudios) return;
                cat.estudios.forEach(function(item, eIdx) {
                    flatCatalog.push({
                        ...item,
                        grupoNombre: grupo.titulo,
                        categoriaNombre: cat.nombre,
                        gIdx: gIdx,
                        cIdx: cIdx,
                        eIdx: eIdx
                    });
                });
            });
        });
        sortFlatCatalog();
```

```
        var tabsContainer = document.getElementById('builder-tabs');
        tabsContainer.innerHTML = '';
        catalogTree.forEach((g, idx) => {
            var btn = document.createElement('button');
            btn.className = 'btn btn-group-tab ' + (idx === currentGroupIndex ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-secondary');
            btn.type = 'button';
            btn.dataset.gidx = idx;
            btn.draggable = true;
            btn.textContent = 'Grupo ' + (idx + 1) + ': ' + (g.titulo ? g.titulo.split('—')[0] : 'Nuevo');
            btn.onclick = function() {
                saveCurrentDOMToTree();
                currentGroupIndex = idx;
                renderTabs();
                renderGroupContent(idx);
            };
            
            // Drag & Drop for Groups
            btn.addEventListener('dragstart', function(e) {
                e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', idx);
                e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
                e.target.style.opacity = '0.5';
            });
            btn.addEventListener('dragend', function(e) {
                e.target.style.opacity = '1';
                tabsContainer.querySelectorAll('.btn-group-tab').forEach(b => b.style.border = '');
            });
            btn.addEventListener('dragover', function(e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';
                e.target.style.borderLeft = '3px solid var(--primary)';
            });
            btn.addEventListener('dragleave', function(e) {
                e.target.style.borderLeft = '';
            });
            btn.addEventListener('drop', function(e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.target.style.borderLeft = '';
                var fromIdx = parseInt(e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain'));
                var toIdx = idx;
                if(fromIdx !== toIdx && !isNaN(fromIdx)) {
                    saveCurrentDOMToTree();
                    var movedGroup = catalogTree.splice(fromIdx, 1)[0];
                    catalogTree.splice(toIdx, 0, movedGroup);
                    currentGroupIndex = toIdx;
                    renderTabs();
                    renderGroupContent(currentGroupIndex);
                }
            });
            
            tabsContainer.appendChild(btn);
        });

        // Añadir botón para nuevo grupo
        var btnAddGroup = document.createElement('button');
        btnAddGroup.className = 'btn btn-outline';
        btnAddGroup.type = 'button';
        btnAddGroup.textContent = '+ Añadir Grupo';
        btnAddGroup.onclick = function() {
            saveCurrentDOMToTree();
            catalogTree.push({ clave: 'G' + (catalogTree.length + 1), titulo: 'Nuevo Grupo', categorias: [] });
            currentGroupIndex = catalogTree.length - 1;
            renderTabs();
            renderGroupContent(currentGroupIndex);
        };
        tabsContainer.appendChild(btnAddGroup);
    }

    function renderGroupContent(idx) {
        var g = catalogTree[idx];
        if (!g) return;
        
        var html = `
            <div class="form-group" style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
                <div style="flex-grow:1; margin-right:1rem;">
                    <label class="form-label">Título Público del Grupo</label>
                    <input type="text" class="form-input form-input--bg" id="cg-titulo" value="${g.titulo.replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}">
                    <input type="hidden" id="cg-clave" value="${g.clave}">
                    <input type="hidden" id="cg-id" value="${g.id}">
                </div>
                <div style="padding-top:1.5rem;">
                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline" style="color:var(--danger); border-color:var(--danger);" id="btn-delete-group">Eliminar Grupo Completo</button>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div id="categorias-container" style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap: 1rem;">
        `;
        
        var groupOptions = catalogTree.map((gObj, gIdx) => `<option value="${gIdx}" ${gIdx === idx ? 'selected' : ''}>${(gObj.titulo || ('Grupo '+(gIdx+1))).replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}</option>`).join('');

        (g.categorias || []).forEach((c, cIdx) => {
            var estCount = c.estudios ? c.estudios.length : 0;
            html += `
                <details class="builder-cat-card" data-cid="${c.id || ''}" draggable="true" style="border:1px solid var(--border); padding:1rem; border-radius:6px; background:#fff; cursor:move;">
                    <summary style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; margin-bottom:1rem; cursor:pointer;">
                        <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0.5rem; width:100%;">
                            <span style="font-weight:bold; color:var(--primary);">📁 Categoría:</span>
                            <input type="text" class="form-input cat-nombre" value="${c.nombre.replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}" style="max-width:250px; padding:4px;" onclick="event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation();" oninput="event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation();">
                            <span class="cat-count" style="color:#666; font-size:0.9rem;">(${estCount})</span>
                            <select class="form-input cat-group-select" data-cidx="${cIdx}" style="max-width:200px; padding:4px; margin-left:1rem; font-size:0.8rem;" onclick="event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation();">
                                ${groupOptions}
                            </select>
                        </div>
```

</details>
